You should also make sure that your phone number and email address have been verified on Venmo. Learn more about phone and email verification on Venmo.

 

I was expecting a payment from a merchant but didn’t receive it

Make sure your phone number and email address are verified.

If that doesn’t resolve the issue, reach out to the merchant directly for assistance. In the case that the merchant is unable to provide a resolution, please feel free to contact Venmo Support for more information.

 

Is there a fee to transfer to my Venmo account?

No. Venmo does not charge users a fee to transfer money into their Venmo account.

Learn more about fees on Venmo.

 

How long will it take for the funds to appear in my Venmo account?

Timing is dependent on the merchant’s process and all transactions may be subject to review, which could result in transfers being delayed or blocked entirely. Once the merchant initiates your payment, funds will be available in your Venmo account immediately. Payment initiation varies by merchant, so please contact the merchant for more details on timing.

Schedule Send Payments

Want to set a scheduled payment for recurring payments to your friends and family? Keep reading this article for FAQs and how-to instructions.  

How do I create a scheduled payment or request?

To set up a scheduled payment, you must ensure you have access to the Venmo app. Once in the app, follow the steps below to complete the setup. 

  1. Tap Pay/Request at the bottom of the screen
  2. Search and add the person you want to pay or charge
  3. Enter an amount
  4. Tap Schedule 
  5. Select your payment frequency 
  6. Select an End Date or No End Date 
  7. Tap Save 
  8. Tap Pay 

Review the screen and verify your payment or request is correct before tapping Schedule Payment. You will receive an email providing you with the details of your scheduled payment. 

How do I edit or cancel my scheduled payments or requests?

You can make changes to your scheduled payment or request from right within the app. Follow the steps below to make updates to your payment.  

  1. Go to the Me tab
  2. Tap Transactions 
  3. Under Scheduled, tap View all
  4. Tap the payment you want to edit; once the payment is open, tap Edit 
  5. Make your changes when finished, tap Update Schedule 

These new changes will take effect on your next scheduled payment or request. 

How do I change the backup payment method for my scheduled payment? 

While creating your scheduled payment, you will be prompted to select a backup payment method you want this recurring payment to come from if there is not enough money in your balance. 

If you need to change your previously selected payment method, follow the steps below. 

  1. Go to the Me tab
  2. Tap Transactions 
  3. Under Scheduled, tap View all
  4. Once the payment is open, tap Edit
  5. Tap Venmo balance or backup, select the new payment method
  6. Tap Update Schedule

Where can I see a list of all my scheduled payments or requests?

You can keep track of all your scheduled payments or requests within the app. Start by opening your Venmo app and follow the steps below. 

  1. Go to the Me tab
  2. Tap Transactions 
  3. Under Scheduled, tap View all

You can select the scheduled payment or request from here, review the details, or make any changes.  

How long do I have to modify a payment or request/ When is the last time I can change my scheduled payment or request?

Any changes to your scheduled payment or request must be completed before 11:59 PM PT the day before your payment or request is due to process. 

I set my scheduled payment, but it wasn’t pulled from my bank until a few days later. 

Using your bank account as a payment method can take up to 5 business days for the money to be processed from your bank. 

If you want your payment pulled from your bank immediately, you can change your payment method to a debit card. This will cause the payment to be pulled from your bank right away. 

I don’t want the scheduled payment to use my balance; how can I select my bank/card only?

If you’ve verified your identity, your Venmo balance will fund any scheduled payments for which the total amount of the payment is available in your balance during processing. While creating your scheduled payment, you will be prompted to select a backup payment method in case there is not enough money in the balance to cover your payment at the time of processing.

If you haven’t verified your identity when creating a scheduled payment, you will be prompted to select a payment method while creating your scheduled payment. Your scheduled payments will be funded by your selected payment method unless you verify your identity and have enough money in your balance to cover your payment at the time of processing.

Sending & Requesting Money

Getting familiar with us this article will help you understand sending or requesting money on Venmo.

 

How can I send a payment or request money with Venmo?

Here’s how to send or request money from the Venmo app:

  1. Tap Pay/Request at the bottom of the screen
  2. Use the Search bar to add the person you want to pay or charge, using their name, Venmo username, phone number, or email address
  3. Enter an amount and add a note
  4. Tap Request or Pay

To quickly pay or charge one of your top payment partners, you can tap their avatar at the top of the Home tab in your Venmo app.

Note: Before you send a payment to someone’s phone number or email address, have the recipient confirm that the number or email is verified on their Venmo account. This can help ensure that your payment reaches the correct account.

You can also send payments and requests after signing in to your Venmo account on the web. Select Pay or Request from the sidebar and follow the above steps.

 

How can I pay multiple people at once?

To pay more than one person at a time, you’ll need to add everyone as a recipient before sending your payment.

Here’s how:

  1. Tap the Pay/Request button at the bottom of your screen
  2. Search for or tap on the name of your first recipient
  3. Next to the first recipient’s name, tap the plus icon (+)
  4. Search for and select your remaining payment partners
  5. Tap “Next” at the bottom of your screen
  6. Enter the amount of money you’d like to pay each person and a payment note
  7. Tap “Pay” or “Request”

There are a few things you’ll want to remember about sending payments to more than one person:

  • The same payment note will be visible to all of each your payment partners
  • You’ll want to make separate payments for differing payment notes

Do I need to add money to Venmo to pay someone?

Nope! You don’t need to add money to Venmo to make payments. If you’re unsure about which payment method will be used for a specific payment, you can always check the bottom of the screen where you enter all your payment details to see which payment method will be used.

Learn more about money in your Venmo account and Venmo balance.

If you don’t see Venmo Balance as an option, you’ll need to verify your identity. Learn more about verifying your identity on Venmo.

I’m having trouble splitting a payment or request.

Before sending the payment, you can edit the total or the individual amounts to make sure each person receives the amount you want. If you receive an error message, the amounts you added may not match. Clear out your inputs and try again.

Splitting & Sharing Purchases

After you complete a purchase with Venmo, you’ll be prompted with the option to split or share your purchase. You can also split or share purchases from your personal transactions feed in the Venmo app.

 

Can I split my purchases?

You can send charge requests to split a purchase with one or many users, and you can change the amount requested from each user so the purchase isn’t evenly split. You’ll be able to see the total amount you’ll request as you change the amount requested from each user, and whether the requested total is above or below the split amount. (Any split transaction will not affect your original payment amount.) You also have the option of excluding yourself from the split calculation. When you split a purchase, you’ll send charge requests to other users. The other users must accept the request in order to complete the payment.

Learn more about sending or requesting money with multiple recipients.

 

Can I cancel a split request?

If you need to cancel a pending payment request from a split, go to the Me tab and find the request at the top of your Transactions feed. From here, you can cancel a split request or remind the other user(s) about the request.

 

Are my purchases private?

As with payments on Venmo, you can set the privacy level for each individual purchase. All purchases default to the Private privacy setting so you can choose which you want to share and when. You can share a purchase (which will change the privacy settings on the purchase) by tapping on it from your personal transactions feed under the Me tab and selecting Share. If you do choose to share your activity, we will always keep the purchase amount private.

Signing Up & Signing In

How to Sign Up for a Personal Venmo Account

Want to get started on Venmo? Check out this article to learn how to sign up for a personal Venmo account.

If you want to sign up for a personal account and a business profile simultaneously, see this article.

First, make sure you meet all the requirements for signing up for Venmo. Then, follow the instructions below to sign up for Venmo:

  1. Download one of our mobile apps: iOS & Android (Venmo does not have a Windows app)
  2. Open the Venmo app
  3. Choose your sign-up method and create a secure password (between 8 and 20 characters long, and should contain at least one number or symbol like !()@#$%
  4. Verify your phone number and email address 
  5. Add and verify your bank account 

After completing the steps above, you should be all set to start receiving payments.

You can also sign up for Venmo from your computer.

Requirements

If you want to use Venmo, make sure you meet all the requirements. Check out this article for more info. 

What are the requirements for using Venmo?

There are a few requirements for using Venmo, and additional products and services such as the Venmo Mastercard Debit Card and Teen Account may have additional eligibility criteria: 

  • You must be physically located in the United States 
  • You must have a U.S.-based cell phone that can send and receive text messages from short codes. This number can’t be on file with another Venmo account. 
  • You must be at least 18 years old (or the age of majority in your state)
  • Your Venmo app must be updated to version 7.38.2 or newer 

You can add a bank account or debit card to your account to transfer the money you receive out of Venmo. You may also want to add a bank account, debit card, or credit card for making payments.

 

What mobile operating systems is the Venmo app compatible with? 

Venmo is only able to support Apple iOS 14 or Android Lollipop (API 26) or higher devices at this time.

Signing In - FAQ

Need help logging in to your existing account? Read on for frequently asked questions about signing in. 

If you’re having trouble accessing your Venmo account, please DON’T create a new one. Doing so can cause issues with your payment methods, and per the User Agreement, you’re only allowed one personal account.

Please keep in mind that business profiles share the same login credentials as personal accounts (rather than having a separate login for each).

 

What information can I use to sign in to my Venmo account?

You can sign in to your Venmo account using the email, phone number, or username that you signed up with, along with your password.

 

I didn’t receive my code to sign in.

Learn more about getting a code to sign into your Venmo account. 

 

I’m having trouble with my password.

Please keep in mind that business profiles share the same login credentials as personal accounts (rather than having a separate login for each). 

Choose “Forgot your password?” or “Forgot Password?” when signing in and enter your email address or phone number. We’ll send you a link to reset your password. You can find more information about the process here. 

 

I’m getting an error message when I try to sign in.

Please make sure you meet all the requirements for using Venmo.

Signing Up for a Personal Account - FAQ

Looking to get started with a personal Venmo account? Read on for some frequently asked questions. 

 

Note: If you are new to Venmo and wish to sign up for a Venmo business profile, you can do so at the same time you apply for your personal account (as you must have a personal account as well.)

If you just intend to sign up for a personal account alone, use the below article.

If you’d like to create a business profile, learn more about signing up for a business profile. 

 

Can I use Venmo outside of the United States?

While we hope to make Venmo available internationally, Venmo is only accessible within the United States at this time. Learn about the requirements for using Venmo. 

 

Why does it say my phone number or email is already on file?

It’s possible that you may have already created a Venmo profile and simply need to sign in. If that is the case, you can use your email, phone number, or username and password to log into your account.

Still not able to log in? Learn more about what to do if your phone number or email is already registered. 

 

Do I need to add a debit card at sign-up?

To help you get the most out of your Venmo experience, we sometimes give you the offer to add a debit card when you create your Venmo account. If you don’t want to add a card right now, or if you want to add something other than a debit card, you can tap Not now or Try later instead. If you decide to add a payment method later, just go to the Settings menu to get started.  

 

Why am I being asked to provide my legal name?

Venmo is a regulated money transfer service, so there are times when we need to verify the identity of our users. We collect your legal name when you sign up so you can get a head start on the identity verification process, which is primarily what this information is used for.

We understand that you may not use your legal name or not want others to see it on your profile, so you’ll also be able to use a display name of your choosing on Venmo.

Learn more about identity verification here.

Supporting Businesses & Charities

Business and Charity Profile Venmo QR Kits

We are currently out of stock and cannot take orders right now. You can still download and print your business QR code from the Venmo app. 

Business and Charity Profiles Passcodes Authentication

Learn how to set up profile Passcodes and limit access to your personal and business or charity profiles.

 

As a business profile or charity profile owner, you can create a secondary Passcodes to limit who can access your personal and business or charity profiles from your Venmo app.

You can also create a Passcodes to be used whenever Venmo is opened on your device. Learn more about setting up a Passcodes/Face ID for your Venmo app.

 

How do profile Passcodes work?

Creating a Passcodes for your profiles can help keep your information private, especially if you have trusted employees who may need to use your device and access your business or charity profile in person. With a Passcodes enabled for your personal profile, you can have some peace of mind while your team collects payments or donations at your next event.

If you choose to enable Passcodes for your profiles, you’ll be required to enter that Passcodes anytime you open Venmo or switch between your profiles. You can opt to enable a Passcodes for either one of your profiles, or both of them. 

Passcodes are unique to each device, so you may need to enable this feature again if you use more than one device to access your Venmo account.

  • Note: If you have Face ID or a similar biometric passcode enabled on your device, this will be used first, in place of your Passcodes. If Face ID fails, you’ll be required to enter the Passcodes for the profile you’re trying to access.

When you re-open Venmo after leaving the app, you’ll be brought back to the profile you accessed most recently. If you’ve enabled a Passcodes for that profile, you’ll be asked to enter it before you can proceed. You’ll also be able to toggle to your other profile instead if needed.

Profile Passcodes are disabled when you sign out of Venmo. To re-enable a Passcodes for your Venmo app and profiles, you’ll need to turn the feature back on after signing in. 

 

How can I create or update my Passscodes?

Follow these steps in your Venmo app to get started or to reset your Passcodes:

  1. Go to the Me tab by tapping your picture or initials 
  2. Tap the Settings gear at the top of your screen 
  3. Select Face ID & Passcodes (iOS) or Passcodes & biometric unlock (Android) 
  4. Tap the toggle to enable or disable the Passcode feature 
  5. Create and confirm your Passcode

Note: Profile Passcode are disabled when you sign out of Venmo. To re-enable a Passcode for your Venmo app and profiles, you’ll need to turn the feature back on after signing in.  

If you forget your Passcode, you’ll need to uninstall and reinstall Venmo on your device.

 

Can I create multiple Passcodes for my profiles?

No. When you enable Passcode and Face ID for Venmo, we’ll only ask you to create a single Passcode. You’ll use the same Passcode (or Face ID / biometric unlock) to access your Venmo app and switch between your profiles.  

 

What if I use multiple devices?

Please note that profile Passcodes are unique to your device. If you use more than one device to access your Venmo account, you will need to enable this feature on each device.

Business and Charity Profiles Photo Gallery FAQ

If you’ve created a business profile or charity profile on Venmo, you can add photos to help enhance your profile and tell the story of your brand or organization.

 

What is the photo gallery for?

Once you’ve created your business or charity profile, you’ll have the opportunity to add photos to your profile. 

For businesses, utilizing your photo gallery can be a great way to build interest for new customers. You can upload your favorite photos to highlight your latest products or service offerings and even create captions for each photo you add to your profile.

For charities, we invite you to add photos that illustrate the causes you help support. You can also use your photo gallery to show how your charity gets involved or tell a story about your latest fundraising efforts.

Your photo gallery is just one more way to help your profile stand out on Venmo—it’s up to you to decide what to do with it!

 

How can I add photos to my gallery?

To add new images to your photo gallery, you’ll first want to grant Venmo permission to access your camera and photos (if you haven’t done so already). Here’s what’s next:

  1. From the Profile tab (of your business or charity profile), tap Add photo
  2. Select the photo you want to add or tap the camera icon to take a new picture
  3. If desired, add a caption to your photos – feel free to get creative!
  4. Tap Upload

If the upload is successful, you’ll see your photos in a new “Photo gallery” section on your business or charity profile.

 

How can I add or edit photo captions?

After opening your photo gallery, select the photo you want to edit and follow these steps:

  1. Tap on the three dots in the upper-right corner of your screen 
  2. Select Edit caption from the popup 
  3. Add or edit the caption as desired, then tap Done 
  4. Tap Done again to save your caption

 

How can I delete photos?

After opening your photo gallery, select the photo you want to delete and follow these steps:

  1. Select the photo you want to delete  
  2. Tap on the three dots in the upper-right corner of your screen 
  3. Select Delete photo and then tap Delete to confirm

If the deletion was successful, you’ll be returned to your photo gallery and will see a confirmation message at the top of your screen. 

 

How many photos can I add to my gallery?

At this time, you can upload a maximum of 10 photos to your business or charity profile. If you’ve already added 10 photos and want to upload a new one, you’ll first need to delete a photo from your profile. 

 

How can I report an inappropriate photo on a Venmo business or charity profile?

We hope you don’t come across inappropriate content from other businesses or charities on Venmo, but, we’ve made it easy to file a report if needed. On the profile or photo you wish to report, tap the three dots in the right-hand corner and select the “Report” option.

You’ll then be taken to a form hosted by our parent company, PayPal. Make sure “Venmo Business Profile” is selected and fill out the form to the best of your ability. Tap Send Email when you’re finished, and we’ll take it from there.

Buying from Business Profiles on Venmo

This article covers all you need to know when paying a business profile from your Venmo app (or on the Venmo website). You can also pay business profiles outside of Venmo, with contactless payments using a compatible digital wallet or debit or credit card. Learn more about paying business profiles with contactless payments.

 

Can I add a tip when I pay a business profile?

Business profile owners decide whether to accept tips for payments sent to their profile. If you’re prompted to add a tip, you can select a preset amount or enter a custom amount to add to your payment total. Please note, the value of your tip can not exceed the lower of 5 times the original payment amount or $1000.

The full amount of your payment (plus any tip you’ve added) will be charged to a single payment method.

 

How can I pay a business profile?

Paying a business profile is as easy as paying your friends on Venmo. There are just a few important things to keep in mind to make sure your payment is sent to the right place: 

  • Search for the business profile using their name or username. Phone numbers and email addresses can’t be used to locate or pay a business on Venmo. 
  • After initiating a search, you’ll see a few results for Top Businesses, which will only include business profiles matching your query. You can tap on See more businesses or select the Businesses tab from the results page to view additional businesses. The People tab will only show personal profiles. 
  • If the business you want to pay has shared their QR code, you can alternatively go to the Me tab and tap the QR code next to your profile picture to scan their code. If the business has created a preset QR code, you’ll see an amount pre-filled for the payment on your screen. Otherwise, you can enter the amount you’ve agreed to pay and add a tip or relevant discounts before sending. 

When you pay a business profile on Venmo, you’ll have an opportunity to customize the payment note and thank the business or provide a testimonial. If you share payment activity publicly or with friends, they’ll see your payment highlighted in the Venmo feed. 

If you experience any issues when trying to pay a business on Venmo, we recommend first reaching out to the business to see if they have more information.  If the issue persists, contact us so we can take a closer look. 

 

Is sales tax included when I pay a business profile on Venmo?

When a business profile sends you a payment request, that business has the option of including sales tax and other applicable tax rates in the payment request. This doesn’t mean that you’ll see an amount different from what you intended to pay, but rather that the business has designated part of your payment total for tax purposes.

If a business sends you a payment request with sales tax included, you’ll see “(includes sales tax)” prior to completing the request.

It’s not required for businesses to use this feature on Venmo, so not every payment you send or request you fulfill will have sales tax included.

 

How can I see whether sales tax was included in my purchase total?

Anytime a business profile has applied sales tax to a payment request, you’ll see “(includes sales tax)” alongside the request notification in your Venmo app.

To confirm whether sales tax was included on a past payment, you can also tap on that completed payment from your personal transactions feed or check the confirmation email you received after sending the payment. Under the Amount sent section of your payment details, you’ll find the original amount of your payment along with any tax rates applied by the business.

 

A business charged me sales tax on a purchase that should be tax-exempt

If you believe that sales tax was included in error on a payment request you received from a business profile, we recommend getting in contact with the business.

Venmo is not responsible for nor determines sales taxes applied by a merchant. Buyers should not contact PayPal or Venmo for sales tax billed or omitted by a merchant.

 

Can I edit the payment note after I pay a business profile?

Payments you send to business profiles are automatically highlighted in the feed if you choose to share your activity publicly or with friends, so be sure to think about what to include in the note before completing your payment.  

If you scanned a business’s QR code to pay, you’ll have a chance to edit and share your payment note after completing the payment. Once your note is shared, that’s it! 

 

How can I update who sees payments I send to business profiles?

You can always choose to update the privacy settings for an individual payment before sending it. To do so, look for and tap on Private, Friends, or Public above the Pay button. From here, you can choose a different privacy setting for that specific payment. 

Privacy settings can also be updated after a payment is completed, or you can adjust your default privacy preference in Settings. You can learn more about sharing and privacy on Venmo here. 

 

How can I get refunded for a payment I sent to a business profile?

All you’ll need to do is contact the business to request a refund. It’s best to contact the business through any existing communications you may have with them, or you can locate their contact information on their Venmo profile. 

Refunds are sent back to the original method used for payment. If you paid a business using money in your Venmo account, your refund will be added to your account once it’s issued by the business. If you paid using a bank account or card, please allow up to 5 business days for a refund to process. 

Please keep in mind that you will not be able to request money from a business profile. 

 

Why am I seeing a business feature?

Venmo is providing the opportunity for business profiles to be featured in your feed. We are always looking for beneficial ways to highlight local businesses near you. Since you chose to share your location with Venmo, you are seeing this business feature and may see more in the future.

When viewing these features in the app you will be able to block the business or report an inappropriate post right from the feed. You always have control over your settings and can find more information about your location settings in our Help Center.

Sending Donations FAQ

Learn how to connect with your favorite charities on Venmo and donate right from your Venmo app.

 

How can I find charities on Venmo?

Charity profiles will always stand out if you see them in your Venmo feed – just look for the blue “verified” checkmark . You can also search for charities on Venmo if you’re looking to donate to a specific organization. Here’s how:

  1. Tap the Pay/Request button
  2. In the Search bar, enter the name of the charity you’re looking for
  3. Tap the Charities filter to see verified charity profiles

If you’ve already donated to a specific charity, you’ll also see their profile listed under “Top Charities” before you begin a search.

To browse more charities on Venmo, select Make a donation after tapping the search bar. This takes you to a new page where you can search for charities by name or browse by category. Tap the slider button next to the search bar to apply filters to your search. From here, you can select categories and causes you want to support.

 

How can I donate to a Venmo charity profile?

If you’ve found a verified charity you want to donate to, all you’ll need to do is tap the Donate button on their profile to begin your donation. Then, follow these steps to complete the process:

  1. Select a preset donation amount or enter an amount using your keyboard
  2. Enter a note for your donation
  3. Tap Donate
  4. We’ll redirect you to your web browser to process your donation
  5. Tap Donate again

Next, you can return to Venmo or share your activity externally, via text, email, or social media. If your Venmo activity is shared publicly or with friends, they’ll see your donation and note in the Venmo feed.

 

Why is there a blue checkmark on this charity profile?

The blue checkmark next to a charity’s name shows you that the charity has verified their status with PayPal, who confirms that information with the IRS. This checkmark should give some peace of mind that you’re donating to an authorized, registered charity on Venmo.

Personal profiles and business profiles won’t have a blue checkmark, so you can always confirm that you’re donating to a verified charity when you see the blue checkmark.

 

Are there any fees to donate to charities on Venmo?

Donation fees are charged to the charity profile rather than the donor. If you donate using a credit card, you will be exempt from Venmo’s standard 3% fee on payments funded by credit cards.

Learn more about fees on Venmo.

 

I can’t find the charity profile I’m looking for

If you’re looking for a specific charity, be sure to select the Charities filter after entering your search.

If you still do not see the charity after searching for them by name, you may want to contact that charity directly to confirm that they’re on Venmo.

Charity profiles can also share their Venmo QR code with you. If they’ve shared their code, you can scan it with your Venmo app and go to their profile.

 

I’m having an issue with my donation

If your donation won’t go through, your connection may have timed out. We recommend trying again later or switching your connection from cellular data to Wi-Fi (if you’re using a mobile device). If you’re still having trouble, please contact our Support team so we can take a closer look.

 

Will I receive a donation receipt after donating to a Venmo charity profile?

After you send a donation, Venmo will issue a transaction receipt to you and the charity. In the transaction receipt, charities will be given the email address associated with your Venmo account, where they can send a written acknowledgment or receipt of donation if applicable.

We recommend contacting the charity directly with any further questions you have about donation receipts.

 

How can I spread the word about my favorite charities on Venmo?

After completing a donation, tap the Share button to share a charity profile with your networks outside of Venmo. Here, you can share profiles via text, email, social media, and more. You can likewise tap the 3 dots at the top of a charity’s profile, then tap Share charity.

Depending on your privacy settings, you can also share a charity profile in your Venmo feed.

  • If your activity is private, you can invite specific friends to donate. Those friends will receive a notification in their Venmo app, which takes them right to the charity profile you shared.
  • If your activity is public or shared with friends, your Venmo network will see your donation in the feed. Donations are highlighted in the feed and include the charity name, photo, and a button for friends to make their own donations.

Learn more about updating your privacy settings.

Tap to Pay for Business Profiles

Accepting Payments with Tap to Pay on Android

How can I accept contactless payments with Tap to Pay on Android?

Once you’ve completed setup for Tap to Pay on Android, tap Charge, then Tap to Pay to start a charge. Then, follow these steps:

  1. At the top of your screen, tap the ($0) amount shown and enter the amount you want to charge your customer
  2. Review and edit any sales taxes you want to be applied to the charge, or tap Add tax to set up and apply a new tax rate if needed
  3. If you want to, enter a note for the charge
    • We recommend entering something to describe or identify the transaction, should you need to find it in the future. The charge note will also be visible on your customer’s receipt.
  4. Tap Charge $X and wait for the next screen to load
  5. Ask the customer to hold their card or device to the back of your phone until you see the “Approved” confirmation on your screen
    • In some cases, customers will be prompted to enter the PIN for their card to complete the charge
  6. From the next screen, choose whether to send your customer a receipt via text message or email and ask them to provide their information

You’re all set! Please note that payments accepted with Tap to Pay may take up to 30 minutes to process and settle into your business profile balance.

Need more help? See how to accept contactless payments with Tap to Pay on Android:

 

Can I manually enter card numbers if Tap to Pay isn’t working?

No. At this time, contactless payments can only be accepted using a customer’s physical card or digital wallet. If your customer doesn’t have a supported contactless payment method, you can still accept payments from Venmo users in the app. Learn more about other ways to accept payments in person.

 

How can I refund a payment I accepted with Tap to Pay?

To refund your customer for a Tap to Pay payment, start by locating their payment in your Venmo app. If your customer shared a QR code from their payment receipt, you can scan this code with your phone’s camera to open the original payment in your Venmo app. On the payment details screen, tap Refund to proceed.

If you don’t have sufficient funds in your business profile balance to cover a refund, we’ll ask you to add money to your business profile balance. You may have the option to add money to your business profile balance from an eligible debit card or verified bank account.

  • Alternatively, you can move funds from your personal profile balance to your business profile balance to cover the refund amount.

To issue a partial refund for a smaller amount, you can tap Refund a smaller amount and edit the refund amount.

Please note the following when attempting to add money to your business profile for Tap to Pay refunds:

  • Money added to your business profile balance with a debit card will be available to you right away
  • Money added to your business profile with a bank account will take 3-5 business days to process and become available to you on Venmo
  • You may be able to add up to $3,000 per week from a connected debit card, or up to $10,000 per week from a verified bank account
  • This feature is currently only available when you refund a contactless transaction from your business profile
  • These types of transfers are subject to review and can be declined for various reasons (including, but not limited to, dollar-amount limits)

When you’re ready to issue your refund, you may need to tap Refund again. Then, tap Refund $X now to send the refund back to your customer’s original payment method. The money should arrive back at your customer’s original payment within 7 days from when you issue the refund.

Once the refund is issued, you and your customer will each receive a notification via email.

Learn more about how refunds work for Venmo business profiles.

 

Why do I need to provide a contact address for receipts?

Providing a business address on customer receipts is an industry requirement for card payments. Because customers who pay you using Tap to Pay don’t need to have a Venmo account, we need to provide them with some contact info to reach your business.

If your business is “mobile” or you do not have a physical store location, you can use geolocation instead of your business address for receipts. If you choose this option, we’ll pull the city and state of your location when you send a receipt to a customer. You can make changes to this selection after tapping “Edit Profile” from your business profile in the Venmo app.

 

Why do I need to wait for my Tap to Pay payments to settle?

When you accept payments with Tap to Pay, they’re processed a little differently from payments sent to you from other Venmo users. It can take up to 30 minutes for us to communicate with your customer’s bank and get the funds into your business profile balance. We’ll let you know as soon as possible if there’s any issue with getting the funds to your account.

 

How can I send a new receipt for a Tap to Pay payment?

If you skipped the option to send a receipt upon completing a charge (or if you need to re-send one), you can do so right in your Venmo app. Here’s how:

  1. Locate and tap on the payment in question from your transaction history
  2. Tap Send receipt
  3. Enter your customer’s email address
  4. Tap Done

That’s it! Your customer will receive an email with their transaction details, including the payment total and any sales tax applied, your business name & contact info, and a QR code that they can share with you to request a refund if needed.

 

How can I view all of the payments I’ve accepted with Tap to Pay?

Contactless payments you accept with Tap to Pay will appear alongside your other activity in your business profile’s transaction history. Rather than seeing another Venmo user as the sender of the payment, you’ll see the last 4 digits of the card your customer used for Tap to Pay payments.

If there’s a specific payment you want to locate, tap the magnifying glass when viewing your business profile transactions in the Venmo app. You can then enter the last 4 digits of your customer’s card number to search for the payment.

You can also view and download your transaction history as a CSV from the Venmo website. Sign in to your account, then visit account.venmo.com/statement and select your business profile using the dropdown at the top of the page.

Accepting Payments with Tap to Pay on iPhone

How can I accept contactless payments with Tap to Pay on iPhone?

Once you’ve completed setup for Tap to Pay on iPhone, you can tap Charge, then Tap to Pay on iPhone to start a charge. Then, follow these steps:

  1. At the top of your screen, tap the ($0) amount shown and enter the amount you want to charge your customer
  2. Review and edit any sales taxes you want to be applied to the charge, or tap Add tax to set up and apply a new tax rate if desired
  3. If you want to, enter a note for the charge
    • We recommend entering something to describe or identify the transaction, should you need to find it in the future. The charge note will also be visible on your customer’s receipt.
  4. Tap Tap to Pay on iPhone and wait for the next screen to load
  5. Present your phone screen to the customer
    • Tip: You can tap the displayed charge amount to flip your screen, so it’s easier for your customer to read
  6. Ask the customer to hold their card over the top of your iPhone, or if they’re paying with Apple Pay (or another digital wallet), they should hold their device near the top of your iPhone. Wait until you see the “Done” confirmation on your screen.
    • For customers with contactless debit or credit cards, ask the customer to hold their card horizontally on the tap area (this may take a few seconds)
    • For customers with Apple Pay and other compatible digital wallets, ask the customer to hold their device near the tap area at the top of your iPhone
    • In some cases, customers will be prompted to enter the PIN for their card to complete the charge (we won’t store this info)
  7. From the next screen, choose whether to send your customer a receipt via text message or email and ask them to provide their information

You’re all set! Please note that payments accepted with Tap to Pay on iPhone may take up to 30 minutes to process and settle into your business profile balance.

Need more help? See how to accept contactless payments with Tap to Pay on iPhone:
